- **pr-review-batching**: Owns the submission lifecycle for PR review comments. Three operations: (1) ensure-then-append, which stages NEW inline comments as drafts on a pending review, creating one if none exists, without clobbering user-in-flight edits; (2) incident response, which undoes an accidentally published review by deleting its visible comments and recreating as pending; (3) replies to EXISTING review threads, which cannot be staged at all, because the replies endpoint publishes on send and the pending-review endpoint rejects `in_reply_to`; those require the user's explicit confirmation before posting.
